# Zogby is ZAO's authored administrator from campaign history. Reasserting the
# predeclared role after the confederation teardown prevents Clausewitz from
# replacing him with a generated pragmatist when subject caches are rebuilt.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_appoint_zao_administrator = {
	set_politics = { ruling_party = pragmatism elections_allowed = yes }
	promote_character = {
		character = WRK_Bassam_Zogby
		ideology = pragmatism_ideology
	}
	set_country_leader_portrait = {
		ideology = pragmatism
		portrait = GFX_portrait_WRK_Bassam_Zogby
	}
	set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_zao_administrator_repair_v1
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_ibl = {
	IBL = {
		transfer_state = 93
		transfer_state = 94
	}
	93 = { add_core_of = IBL add_claim_by = IBL set_state_controller_to = IBL }
	94 = { add_core_of = IBL add_claim_by = IBL set_state_controller_to = IBL }
	IBL = {
		set_capital = { state = 93 }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ibl_initialized } }
			ADISCORD_grant_2150_technology_baseline = yes
			ADISCORD_grant_technology_profile_fragment_low_tech = yes
			ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_prepare_conflict_country = yes
			load_oob = "IBL_vorkerland_collapse"
			add_manpower = 4500
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 700 producer = IVN }
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ibl_initialized
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_client
	}
	IVN = {
		if = {
			limit = { IBL = { NOT = { is_subject_of = IVN } } }
			puppet = IBL
			set_autonomy = { target = IBL autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet freedom_level = 0.10 }
		}
	}
	IBL = {
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_appoint_selevyostrov = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.42 days = 1 }
	}
	set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_krait_frontier_secured
}

# Preserve the exact northern war graph before the foreign operation. Each edge
# was created as its own war, so each can be safely stopped and restored.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_begin_ivanland_intervention = {
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_zao_wpa
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_zao_wps
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_zao_psd
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_zao_pwr
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wpa_psd
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wpa_pwr
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wps_psd
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wps_pwr
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_pwr_psd
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_wars_restored
	if = { limit = { ZAO = { has_war_with = WPA } } set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_zao_wpa }
	if = { limit = { ZAO = { has_war_with = WPS } } set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_zao_wps }
	if = { limit = { ZAO = { has_war_with = PSD } } set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_zao_psd }
	if = { limit = { ZAO = { has_war_with = PWR } } set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_zao_pwr }
	if = { limit = { WPA = { has_war_with = PSD } } set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wpa_psd }
	if = { limit = { WPA = { has_war_with = PWR } } set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wpa_pwr }
	if = { limit = { WPS = { has_war_with = PSD } } set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wps_psd }
	if = { limit = { WPS = { has_war_with = PWR } } set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wps_pwr }
	if = { limit = { PWR = { has_war_with = PSD } } set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_pwr_psd }

	ZAO = {
		if = { limit = { country_exists = WPA has_war_with = WPA } white_peace = WPA }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = WPS has_war_with = WPS } white_peace = WPS }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = PSD has_war_with = PSD } white_peace = PSD }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = PWR has_war_with = PWR } white_peace = PWR }
	}
	WPA = {
		if = { limit = { country_exists = PSD has_war_with = PSD } white_peace = PSD }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = PWR has_war_with = PWR } white_peace = PWR }
	}
	WPS = {
		if = { limit = { country_exists = PSD has_war_with = PSD } white_peace = PSD }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = PWR has_war_with = PWR } white_peace = PWR }
	}
	PWR = {
		if = { limit = { country_exists = PSD has_war_with = PSD } white_peace = PSD }
	}

	# Neither postwar client exists yet. Ivanland first has to occupy all four
	# settlement states; both administrations are created only after victory.
	IVN = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_expedition_supplied } }
			add_manpower = 8000
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 1600 producer = IVN }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 120 producer = IVN }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment amount = 48 producer = IVN }
			add_fuel = 5000
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_expedition_supplied
		}
		# Let every white peace and faction removal cross one diplomatic-cache tick.
		# The short flag distinguishes this saved pre-front window from a genuinely
		# missing war when on_startup repairs an older active intervention.
		set_country_flag = { flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_front_launch_pending days = 4 }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.77 days = 1 }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_end_ivanland_intervention_wars = {
	IVN = {
		if = { limit = { country_exists = ZAO has_war_with = ZAO } white_peace = ZAO }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = WPA has_war_with = WPA } white_peace = WPA }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = WPS has_war_with = WPS } white_peace = WPS }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = PWR has_war_with = PWR } white_peace = PWR }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = PSD has_war_with = PSD } white_peace = PSD }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = IBL has_war_with = IBL } white_peace = IBL }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = IBA has_war_with = IBA } white_peace = IBA }
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_front_launch_pending
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_front_verification_pending
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_front_war_retry
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_front_join_retry
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_front_verified_v1
	}
	PWR = { remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_defense_front if = { limit = { is_faction_leader = yes } dismantle_faction = yes } else_if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes } }
	ZAO = { remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_defense_front if = { limit = { is_faction_leader = yes } dismantle_faction = yes } else_if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes } }
	WPA = { remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_defense_front if = { limit = { is_faction_leader = yes } dismantle_faction = yes } else_if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes } }
	WPS = { remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_defense_front if = { limit = { is_faction_leader = yes } dismantle_faction = yes } else_if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes } }
	PSD = { remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_defense_front if = { limit = { is_faction_leader = yes } dismantle_faction = yes } else_if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes } }
	IBL = { if = { limit = { is_faction_leader = yes } dismantle_faction = yes } else_if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes } }
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_northern_wars = {
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_zao_wpa country_exists = ZAO country_exists = WPA ZAO = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = WPA } } WPA = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } } ZAO = { declare_war_on = { target = WPA type = annex_everything } } }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_zao_wps country_exists = ZAO country_exists = WPS ZAO = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = WPS } } WPS = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } } WPS = { declare_war_on = { target = ZAO type = annex_everything } } }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_zao_psd country_exists = ZAO country_exists = PSD ZAO = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = PSD } } PSD = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } } ZAO = { declare_war_on = { target = PSD type = annex_everything } } }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_zao_pwr country_exists = ZAO country_exists = PWR ZAO = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = PWR } } PWR = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } } PWR = { declare_war_on = { target = ZAO type = annex_everything } } }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wpa_psd country_exists = WPA country_exists = PSD WPA = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = PSD } } PSD = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } } WPA = { declare_war_on = { target = PSD type = annex_everything } } }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wpa_pwr country_exists = WPA country_exists = PWR WPA = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = PWR } } PWR = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } } WPA = { declare_war_on = { target = PWR type = annex_everything } } }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wps_psd country_exists = WPS country_exists = PSD WPS = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = PSD } } PSD = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } } WPS = { declare_war_on = { target = PSD type = annex_everything } } }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wps_pwr country_exists = WPS country_exists = PWR WPS = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = PWR } } PWR = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } } WPS = { declare_war_on = { target = PWR type = annex_everything } } }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_pwr_psd country_exists = PWR country_exists = PSD PWR = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = PSD } } PSD = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } } PWR = { diplomatic_relation = { country = PSD relation = non_aggression_pact active = no } } PWR = { declare_war_on = { target = PSD type = annex_everything } } }
}

# If PWR's just-ended capitulation blocks both surviving saved edges after an
# Ivanland victory, keep the northern civil war alive with one bounded WPA-WPS
# successor conflict.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_surviving_northern_war_fallback = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_succeeded
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wpa_pwr
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wps_pwr
			OR = { NOT = { country_exists = PWR } PWR = { is_subject = yes } PWR = { has_capitulated = yes } }
			NOT = { PWR = { has_war_with = PSD } }
			country_exists = WPA
			country_exists = WPS
			WPA = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = WPS } NOT = { has_war_with = PWR } }
			WPS = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = PWR } }
		}
		WPA = { declare_war_on = { target = WPS type = annex_everything } }
	}
}

# Consume saved edges only after the declaration is visible in diplomacy. A
# rejected viable edge stays set for diagnosis instead of being silently lost.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_verify_northern_war_restore = {
	set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_war_restore_attempted_v2
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_succeeded
			WPA = { has_war_with = WPS }
		}
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wpa_pwr
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wps_pwr
	}
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_zao_wpa OR = { ZAO = { has_war_with = WPA } NOT = { country_exists = ZAO } NOT = { country_exists = WPA } ZAO = { is_subject = yes } WPA = { is_subject = yes } } } clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_zao_wpa }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_zao_wps OR = { ZAO = { has_war_with = WPS } NOT = { country_exists = ZAO } NOT = { country_exists = WPS } ZAO = { is_subject = yes } WPS = { is_subject = yes } } } clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_zao_wps }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_zao_psd OR = { ZAO = { has_war_with = PSD } NOT = { country_exists = ZAO } NOT = { country_exists = PSD } ZAO = { is_subject = yes } PSD = { is_subject = yes } } } clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_zao_psd }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_zao_pwr OR = { ZAO = { has_war_with = PWR } NOT = { country_exists = ZAO } NOT = { country_exists = PWR } ZAO = { is_subject = yes } PWR = { is_subject = yes } } } clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_zao_pwr }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wpa_psd OR = { WPA = { has_war_with = PSD } NOT = { country_exists = WPA } NOT = { country_exists = PSD } WPA = { is_subject = yes } PSD = { is_subject = yes } } } clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wpa_psd }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wpa_pwr OR = { WPA = { has_war_with = PWR } NOT = { country_exists = WPA } NOT = { country_exists = PWR } WPA = { is_subject = yes } PWR = { is_subject = yes } } } clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wpa_pwr }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wps_psd OR = { WPS = { has_war_with = PSD } NOT = { country_exists = WPS } NOT = { country_exists = PSD } WPS = { is_subject = yes } PSD = { is_subject = yes } } } clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wps_psd }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wps_pwr OR = { WPS = { has_war_with = PWR } NOT = { country_exists = WPS } NOT = { country_exists = PWR } WPS = { is_subject = yes } PWR = { is_subject = yes } } } clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wps_pwr }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_pwr_psd OR = { PWR = { has_war_with = PSD } NOT = { country_exists = PWR } NOT = { country_exists = PSD } PWR = { is_subject = yes } PSD = { is_subject = yes } } } clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_pwr_psd }
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_zao_wpa
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_zao_wps
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_zao_psd
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_zao_pwr
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wpa_psd
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wpa_pwr
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wps_psd
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_wps_pwr
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_pwr_psd
				}
			}
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_wars_restored
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_war_restore_v2
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND] Northern wars restored and verified after the bounded retry."
	}
	else = {
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND] Northern war restoration aborted after the bounded retry; unresolved saved edges were preserved."
	}
}

# Legacy create_country_leader does not replace a character-system leader in
# HOI4 1.19. Mateusk is recruited by IBA history, so promote that character
# directly; recruit_character is deliberately forbidden in scripted effects.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_appoint_mateusk = {
	set_politics = { ruling_party = pragmatism elections_allowed = yes }
	promote_character = {
		character = IBA_Matvey_Mateusk
		ideology = pragmatism_ideology
	}
	set_country_leader_portrait = {
		ideology = pragmatism
		portrait = GFX_portrait_IBA_Matvey_Mateusk
	}
	set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_mateusk_character_repair_v2
}

# Krait is a postwar client. Keeping its creation in one effect also makes old
# interrupted settlement saves converge on the same states and government.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_expand_krait_client = {
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_ibl = yes
}

# The final settlement creates the clients in a stable order: Mateusk's Norvane
# receives states 90-91 first, then Krait is materialised in states 93-94.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_ivanland_mandate = {
	IBA = {
		transfer_state = 90
		transfer_state = 91
	}
	90 = { add_core_of = IBA add_claim_by = IBA set_state_controller_to = IBA }
	91 = { remove_core_of = IBL add_core_of = IBA add_claim_by = IBA set_state_controller_to = IBA }
	71 = { add_claim_by = IBA }
	IBA = {
		set_capital = { state = 90 }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_iba_initialized } }
			ADISCORD_grant_2150_technology_baseline = yes
			ADISCORD_grant_technology_profile_fragment_low_tech = yes
			ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_prepare_conflict_country = yes
			load_oob = "IBA_vorkerland_collapse"
			add_manpower = 3500
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 700 producer = IVN }
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_iba_initialized
		}
	}
	IVN = {
		if = {
			limit = { IBA = { NOT = { is_subject_of = IVN } } }
			puppet = IBA
			set_autonomy = { target = IBA autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet freedom_level = 0.10 }
		}
	}
	# Puppeting may rebuild the new country's politics and replace an already
	# promoted leader with a generated one. Appoint Mateusk only after that
	# rebuild, then make one deferred assertion on the following day.
	IBA = {
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_appoint_mateusk = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.40 days = 1 }
	}
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_ibl = yes
	set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_client_order_v2
}

# The visible 120-day preparation is the sole fresh producer of the 240-day
# intervention mission. The mission is non-selectable and activated here so its
# UI deadline and the actual war share one authority.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_activate_ivanland_intervention_mission = {
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_mission_activation_pending_v3
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = IVN
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_wars_began
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_resolved }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_active }
			ADISCORD_ivanland_intervention_start_ready = yes
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_mission_guaranteed_v2
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_mission_activation_v3
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_available
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_active
		add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_expeditionary_command days = 260 }
		activate_mission = ADISCORD_ivanland_limited_intervention
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_begin_ivanland_intervention = yes
	}
}

# The ceasefire and faction teardown performed by begin_ivanland_intervention
# must enter the diplomatic cache before the northern defense front is rebuilt.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_open_ivanland_intervention_front = {
	IVN = {
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_front_verified_v1
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_front_war_retry
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_front_join_retry
	}
	PWR = {
		create_faction_from_template = {
			template = faction_template_ADISCORD_standard
			name = faction_vorkerland_northern_defense_front
		}
		add_to_faction = ZAO
		add_to_faction = WPA
		add_to_faction = WPS
		add_to_faction = PSD
	}
	ZAO = { add_manpower = 1000 add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 250 producer = ZAO } add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_defense_front days = 260 } }
	WPA = { add_manpower = 1000 add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 250 producer = WPA } add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_defense_front days = 260 } }
	WPS = { add_manpower = 1000 add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 250 producer = WPS } add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_defense_front days = 260 } }
	PWR = { add_manpower = 1000 add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 250 producer = PWR } add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_defense_front days = 260 } }
	PSD = { add_manpower = 1000 add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 250 producer = PSD } add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_defense_front days = 260 } }
	# PWR leads the shared defense faction, so one war reaches both PWR and PSD.
	# A take_state_focus generator is scoped to the declared target: 93/94 remain
	# PSD-owned and enter through its explicit defender join below. All four still
	# belong to the scripted occupation postcondition before IBA/IBL materialise.
	IVN = { declare_war_on = { target = PWR type = take_state_focus generator = { 90 91 } } }
	ZAO = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = IVN } } add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = PWR enemy = IVN hostility_reason = asked_to_join } } }
	WPA = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = IVN } } add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = PWR enemy = IVN hostility_reason = asked_to_join } } }
	WPS = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = IVN } } add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = PWR enemy = IVN hostility_reason = asked_to_join } } }
	PSD = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = IVN } } add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = PWR enemy = IVN hostility_reason = asked_to_join } } }
	IVN = {
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_front_launch_pending
		set_country_flag = { flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_front_verification_pending days = 3 }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.82 days = 1 }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_end_rom_northern_intervention_wars = {
	ROM = {
		if = { limit = { country_exists = ZAO has_war_with = ZAO } white_peace = ZAO }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = WPA has_war_with = WPA } white_peace = WPA }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = WPS has_war_with = WPS } white_peace = WPS }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = PWR has_war_with = PWR } white_peace = PWR }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = PSD has_war_with = PSD } white_peace = PSD }
	}
}

# Every live edge schedules at most one near-term state assertion. The temporary
# country flag prevents peace/capitulation callbacks in the same tick from
# multiplying event .46.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_schedule_rom_northern_intervention_check = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = ROM
			ROM = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_check_pending } }
		}
		ROM = {
			set_country_flag = { flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_check_pending days = 3 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.46 days = 1 }
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_schedule_rom_northern_cleanup_verification = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = ROM }
		ROM = {
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_cleanup_pending
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_cleanup_retry
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.46 days = 1 }
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_success = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_resolved }
			ROM = {
				exists = yes
				NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
				controls_state = 72
				controls_state = 196
				controls_state = 322
			}
		}
		# Lock the terminal result before white peace: on_peace may re-enter this
		# chain synchronously and must observe an idempotent outcome.
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_succeeded
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_failed
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_resolved
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_active
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_end_rom_northern_intervention_wars = yes
		ROM = {
			transfer_state = 72
			transfer_state = 196
			transfer_state = 322
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_corridor_secured
		}
		72 = { add_claim_by = ROM set_state_controller_to = ROM }
		196 = { add_claim_by = ROM set_state_controller_to = ROM }
		322 = { add_claim_by = ROM set_state_controller_to = ROM }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_schedule_rom_northern_cleanup_verification = yes
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND] Frealor northern intervention resolved: SUCCESS after control of states 72, 196 and 322."
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_resolved } }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_failure = yes
	}
	else = {
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_end_rom_northern_intervention_wars = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_schedule_rom_northern_cleanup_verification = yes
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_failure = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_resolved } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_failed
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_succeeded
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_resolved
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_active
		ROM = { clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_corridor_secured }
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND] Frealor northern intervention resolved: FAILURE; no target states transferred."
	}
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_end_rom_northern_intervention_wars = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_schedule_rom_northern_cleanup_verification = yes
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_resolve_rom_northern_intervention_timeout = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROM = {
				exists = yes
				NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
				controls_state = 72
				controls_state = 196
				controls_state = 322
			}
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_success = yes
	}
	else = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_failure = yes }
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_check_rom_northern_intervention = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_active
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_resolved }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				ROM = {
					exists = yes
					NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
					controls_state = 72
					controls_state = 196
					controls_state = 322
				}
			}
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_success = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_war_finished
					NOT = { country_exists = ROM }
					ROM = { OR = { is_subject = yes has_capitulated = yes } }
					AND = { ROM = { NOT = { controls_state = 72 } } OR = { NOT = { country_exists = ZAO } ZAO = { has_capitulated = yes } } }
					AND = { ROM = { NOT = { controls_state = 72 } NOT = { has_war_with = ZAO } } }
					AND = { ROM = { OR = { NOT = { controls_state = 196 } NOT = { controls_state = 322 } } } OR = { NOT = { country_exists = WPS } WPS = { has_capitulated = yes } } }
					AND = { ROM = { OR = { NOT = { controls_state = 196 } NOT = { controls_state = 322 } } NOT = { has_war_with = WPS } } }
					ROM = { NOT = { OR = { has_war_with = ZAO has_war_with = WPA has_war_with = WPS has_war_with = PWR has_war_with = PSD } } }
				}
			}
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_northern_intervention_failure = yes
		}
	}
}

# Only Workerland's own victory can restore its former republic autonomies.
# The two rolls are independent. Ivanland's timely guarantee suppresses this
# entire settlement and leaves both republics sovereign.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_resolve_unguaranteed_free_republics = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_free_republics_resolution_done } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_free_republics_guaranteed_by_ivn } }
			WRK = {
				if = {
					limit = { ROM = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_ai = yes NOT = { is_in_faction_with = WRK } } }
					random_list = {
						50 = {
							puppet = ROM
							set_autonomy = { target = ROM autonomy_state = autonomy_republic_in_Vorkerland freedom_level = 0.10 }
							ROM = {
								ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_party_identity = yes
								ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_republics_from_ruins = yes
							}
						}
						50 = {
							if = { limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = ROM } } declare_war_on = { target = ROM type = annex_everything } }
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { TRU = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_ai = yes NOT = { is_in_faction_with = WRK } } }
					random_list = {
						50 = {
							puppet = TRU
							set_autonomy = { target = TRU autonomy_state = autonomy_republic_in_Vorkerland freedom_level = 0.10 }
							TRU = {
								ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_party_identity = yes
								ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_republics_from_ruins = yes
							}
						}
						50 = {
							if = { limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = TRU } } declare_war_on = { target = TRU type = annex_everything } }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		# These breakaway governments are outside the guarantee for ROM and TRU.
		WRK = {
			if = {
				limit = { DVA = { exists = yes is_subject = no has_capitulated = no NOT = { is_in_faction_with = WRK } NOT = { has_war_with = WRK } } }
				declare_war_on = { target = DVA type = annex_everything }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { ZTA = { exists = yes is_subject = no has_capitulated = no NOT = { is_in_faction_with = WRK } NOT = { has_war_with = WRK } } }
				declare_war_on = { target = ZTA type = annex_everything }
			}
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_free_republics_resolution_done
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_success = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_resolved } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_resolved
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervened
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_succeeded
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_mandate_proclaimed
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_failed
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_available
		IVN = {
			remove_mission = ADISCORD_ivanland_limited_intervention
			remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_expeditionary_command
		}
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND] Ivanland intervention resolved: SUCCESS; IBA receives states 90-91 first, then IBL receives states 93-94."
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_end_ivanland_intervention_wars = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_ivanland_mandate = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_end_ivanland_intervention_wars = yes
		# Keep the active guard through white peace and postwar materialisation so a
		# same-tick capitulation cannot fall into the generic annexation fallback.
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_active
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.43 days = 2 }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_success_news_shown } }
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_success_news_shown
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_news.1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_succeeded }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_end_ivanland_intervention_wars = yes
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_failure = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_resolved } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_resolved
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_failed
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_succeeded
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_mandate_proclaimed
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_available
		IVN = {
			remove_mission = ADISCORD_ivanland_limited_intervention
			remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_expeditionary_command
		}
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND] Ivanland intervention resolved: FAILURE; no postwar clients survive and states 90-94 return to the northern republics."
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_end_ivanland_intervention_wars = yes
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = IBA }
			IVN = { annex_country = { target = IBA transfer_troops = yes } }
		}
		# A bounded settlement retry can already find the Krait client materialized.
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = IBL }
			IVN = { annex_country = { target = IBL transfer_troops = yes } }
		}
		90 = { remove_core_of = IBA }
		91 = { remove_core_of = IBA remove_core_of = IBL }
		93 = { remove_core_of = IBL }
		94 = { remove_core_of = IBL }
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = PWR }
			PWR = { transfer_state = 90 transfer_state = 91 }
			90 = { set_state_controller_to = PWR }
			91 = { add_core_of = PWR set_state_controller_to = PWR }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = PSD }
			PSD = { transfer_state = 93 transfer_state = 94 }
			93 = { set_state_controller_to = PSD }
			94 = { set_state_controller_to = PSD }
		}
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_krait_frontier_secured
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_end_ivanland_intervention_wars = yes
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_active
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.43 days = 2 }
		IVN = {
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = etatism
				elections_allowed = no
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vadim_etatist_role_added } }
				add_country_leader_role = {
					character = IVN_Vadim_Ivanchik
					promote_leader = yes
					country_leader = {
						ideology = etatism_ideology
						expire = "2200.1.1.1"
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vadim_etatist_role_added
			}
			set_country_leader_portrait = {
				ideology = etatism
				portrait = GFX_portrait_IVN_Vadim_Ivanchik_after_retreat
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_failure_news_shown } }
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_failure_news_shown
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_news.2 }
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_promote_anton_bagley = {
	add_country_leader_role = {
		character = WRK_Anton_Bagley
		promote_leader = yes
		country_leader = {
			ideology = utilitarian_accelerationism
			expire = "2200.1.1.1"
			traits = { Emergency_Powers }
		}
	}
}

# A transferred character keeps the leader role authored for the old country.
# Re-promoting that role with another ideology can make the engine generate a
# fallback leader instead of activating the exact character. Add Nikita's WKR
# role explicitly, following the vanilla transfer-then-add-role pattern.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_promote_nikita_worcker = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_nikita_wkr_role_added_v2 } }
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = WRK_Nikita_Worcker
			promote_leader = yes
			country_leader = {
				ideology = neo_vorkerism
				expire = "2200.1.1.1"
				traits = { Emergency_Powers }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_nikita_wkr_role_added_v2
	}
	else = {
		promote_character = {
			character = WRK_Nikita_Worcker
			ideology = neo_vorkerism
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_prepare_claimant_characters = {
	# Move only the selected route characters while pre-collapse WRK still owns
	# them. This prevents WKR from receiving a generated fallback leader.
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_safe_with_loyalists }
		WRK = {
			set_nationality = { target_country = WKR character = WRK_Nikita_Worcker }
		}
	}
	else = {
		WRK = {
			set_nationality = { target_country = WKR character = WRK_Anton_Bagley }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_rescued_by_vlad }
		WRK = {
			set_nationality = { target_country = VAD character = WRK_Nikita_Worcker }
			set_nationality = { target_country = VAD character = WRK_VAD_Joint_Council }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_killed }
		WRK = { retire_character = WRK_Nikita_Worcker }
	}
}

# Independent republic cosmetics must never survive a later dependency. This
# shared synchronizer is called at collapse setup and by autonomy on-actions.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_independence_cosmetic = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_started
			OR = { tag = ROM tag = TRU tag = ZAO tag = SOL }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { tag = SOL }
			if = {
				limit = { OR = { is_subject_of = WKR is_subject_of = WRK } }
				set_cosmetic_tag = SOL_vorkerland_worker_protectorate
			}
			else = { drop_cosmetic_tag = yes }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { is_subject = yes }
			drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { tag = ROM }
			set_cosmetic_tag = ROM_frealor_republic
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { tag = TRU }
			set_cosmetic_tag = TRU_zolotorevsk_republic
		}
		else = { set_cosmetic_tag = ZAO_zaozersk_republic }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_apply_claimant_cosmetics = {
	WKR = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_safe_with_loyalists }
			set_politics = { ruling_party = pragmatism elections_allowed = no }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_promote_nikita_worcker = yes
			set_cosmetic_tag = WRK_vorkerland_emergency
			set_country_leader_portrait = {
				ideology = pragmatism
				portrait = GFX_portrait_WRK_Nikita_Worcker_civilwar
			}
		}
		else = {
			# Register Anton's authored utilitarian leader role before switching the
			# ruling party. Otherwise HOI4 immediately fills the vacant utilitarian
			# slot with a generated name from the WRK pool.
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_promote_anton_bagley = yes
			set_politics = { ruling_party = utilitarism elections_allowed = no }
			set_cosmetic_tag = WRK_vorkerland_utilitarian_republic
			set_country_leader_portrait = {
				ideology = utilitarism
				portrait = GFX_portrait_WRK_Anton_Bagley
			}
		}
	}
	VAD = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_joint_government_formed }
			set_cosmetic_tag = WRK_vorkerland_joint_government
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_appoint_joint_council = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_cosmetic_tag = VAD_vorkerland_restoration
			set_country_leader_portrait = {
				ideology = pragmatism
				portrait = GFX_portrait_WRK_Vlad_Petrichev_civilwar
			}
		}
	}
	ROM = {
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_independence_cosmetic = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_appoint_rom_etatist = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.60 days = 1 }
	}
	TRU = {
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_independence_cosmetic = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_appoint_tru_chauvinist = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.61 days = 1 }
	}
	ZAO = {
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_independence_cosmetic = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_appoint_zao_administrator = yes
		load_focus_tree = { tree = ADISCORD_vorkerland_zao_focus keep_completed = yes }
		mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.68 days = 1 }
	}
	SOL = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_independence_cosmetic = yes }
	PWR = {
		set_cosmetic_tag = PWR_rimat_republic
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_appoint_pwr_technocrat = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.44 days = 1 }
	}
	VLA = { set_cosmetic_tag = VLA_volnograd_republic }
}

# Use the same character-system path as Mateusk. This avoids leaving the
# generated pragmatist in office when the joint government is formed.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_appoint_joint_council = {
	set_politics = { ruling_party = pragmatism elections_allowed = no }
	promote_character = {
		character = WRK_VAD_Joint_Council
		ideology = pragmatism_ideology
	}
	set_country_leader_portrait = {
		ideology = pragmatism
		portrait = GFX_portrait_WRK_Temporary_Government
	}
	set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_joint_council_character_repair_v2
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_form_joint_government = {
	# Rare wartime rescue route: the council belongs to VAD while VAD and WKR
	# remain separate. Ordinary VAD victory must keep Vlad and never consume WKR.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_rescued_by_vlad
			country_exists = VAD
			country_exists = WKR
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_joint_government_formed
		VAD = {
			set_cosmetic_tag = WRK_vorkerland_joint_government
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_appoint_joint_council = yes
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.62 days = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# State ownership changes do not reliably path legacy armies out of the newly
# foreign territory. Reassemble the pre-existing forces only after every new
# owner, capital and OOB has been established.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_relocate_legacy_armies = {
	every_state = {
		# WRK's legacy formations move before transfer_troops hands them to WKR.
		# Do not teleport WKR itself: its new state-33 home guard must keep the
		# border deployment authored by ensure_wkr_home_guard.
		teleport_armies = { limit = { tag = WRK } to_state = 32 }
		teleport_armies = { limit = { tag = VAD } to_state = 75 }
		teleport_armies = { limit = { tag = EYR } to_state = 102 }
		teleport_armies = { limit = { tag = ZAO } to_state = 72 }
		teleport_armies = { limit = { tag = PWR } to_state = 71 }
		teleport_armies = { limit = { tag = VLA } to_state = 74 }
		teleport_armies = { limit = { tag = ROM } to_state = 73 }
		teleport_armies = { limit = { tag = SOL } to_state = 76 }
		teleport_armies = { limit = { tag = TRU } to_state = 80 }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_apply_initial_map = {
	WKR = {
		transfer_state = 32
		transfer_state = 33
		# State 40 is impassable, so annex_country does not reliably inherit it
		# from the dormant WRK shell. Transfer it with the other WKR home states.
		transfer_state = 40
		transfer_state = 200
		transfer_state = 201
		set_capital = { state = 32 }
	}
	40 = { set_state_controller_to = WKR }
	VAD = {
		transfer_state = 75
		transfer_state = 106
		transfer_state = 107
		transfer_state = 121
		set_capital = { state = 75 }
	}
	ZAO = { transfer_state = 72 }
	PWR = {
		transfer_state = 71
		transfer_state = 90
		transfer_state = 91
		transfer_state = 202
	}
	91 = { add_core_of = PWR set_state_controller_to = PWR }
	202 = { add_core_of = PWR set_state_controller_to = PWR }
	VLA = { transfer_state = 74 }
	ROM = { transfer_state = 73 }
	SOL = { transfer_state = 76 }
	TRU = { transfer_state = 80 }

	ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_tva = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_wtd = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_eyr = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_egc = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_riv = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_rev = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_yor = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_ndn = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_swb = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_vhv = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_osv = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_csl = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_wpa = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_wps = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_psd = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_eba = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_dva = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_sra = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_zta = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_tgd = yes
	# The External Gate is a WRK emergency dependency, not a civil-war claimant.
	# At the collapse its core-bearing Kremen and Leman administrations replace it.
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_split_external_gate = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_prepare_initial_combatants = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_relocate_legacy_armies = yes
}

# Country-scoped exhaustion. WKR, VAD and TVA update only from bounded war,
# peace and capitulation hooks, so each side owns its variables and losses.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_initialize_civil_war_exhaustion = {
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion value = 1 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_casualties_delta_k value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_casualties_snapshot_k value = casualties_k }
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_civil_war_exhaustion = yes
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_update_civil_war_exhaustion = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_casualties_snapshot_k }
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_casualties_snapshot_k
			value = casualties_k
		}
	}

	set_variable = {
		var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_casualties_delta_k
		value = casualties_k
	}
	subtract_from_variable = {
		var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_casualties_delta_k
		value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_casualties_snapshot_k
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_casualties_delta_k
		min = 0
		max = 10000
	}
	set_variable = {
		var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_casualties_snapshot_k
		value = casualties_k
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_war_finished }
			has_war = yes
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion
			value = 2
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_casualties_delta_k
					value = 100
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion
				value = 6
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_casualties_delta_k
					value = 25
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion
				value = 3
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_casualties_delta_k
					value = 5
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion
				value = 1
			}
		}
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion
		min = 1
		max = 100
	}
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_civil_war_exhaustion = yes
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_civil_war_exhaustion = {
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion_level value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion
				value = 80
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion_level value = 4 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion
				value = 60
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion_level value = 3 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion
				value = 40
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion_level value = 2 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion
				value = 20
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion_level value = 1 }
	}

	set_variable = {
		var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion_war_support_factor
		value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion
	}
	multiply_variable = {
		var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion_war_support_factor
		value = -0.002
	}
	set_variable = {
		var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion_stability_factor
		value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion
	}
	multiply_variable = {
		var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion_stability_factor
		value = -0.001
	}
	set_variable = {
		var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion_industrial_capacity_factory
		value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion
	}
	multiply_variable = {
		var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion_industrial_capacity_factory
		value = -0.001
	}
	set_variable = {
		var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion_army_morale_factor
		value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion
	}
	multiply_variable = {
		var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion_army_morale_factor
		value = -0.0005
	}
	set_variable = {
		var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion_surrender_limit
		value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion
	}
	multiply_variable = {
		var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion_surrender_limit
		value = -0.001
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion
				value = 1
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_dynamic_modifier = {
						modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion
					}
				}
			}
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion }
		}
		force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dynamic_modifier = {
					modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion
				}
			}
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion }
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_reset_civil_war_exhaustion = {
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_exhaustion value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_casualties_delta_k value = 0 }
	set_variable = {
		var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_casualties_snapshot_k
		value = casualties_k
	}
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_civil_war_exhaustion = yes
}

# Capitulation and state-control hooks can fire before a peace conference has
# finished transferring occupations. This guarded checker is safe to call from
# all bounded lifecycle hooks without scheduling duplicate events.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_check_central_outcome = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_war_finished }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_finished }
		}
		# collapse.20/.21/.22 are triggered-only. Without this dispatch a
		# de facto winner never enters apply_*_map or the victory presentation.
		if = {
			limit = { WKR = { exists = yes ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_victory_candidate = yes } }
			WKR = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.20 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { VAD = { exists = yes ADISCORD_vorkerland_vlad_victory_candidate = yes } }
			VAD = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.21 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { TVA = { exists = yes ADISCORD_vorkerland_dorian_victory_candidate = yes } }
			TVA = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.22 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_showdown
						has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_started
						NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_launch_failed }
					}
					WKR = { exists = yes ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_victory_candidate = yes }
					VAD = { exists = yes ADISCORD_vorkerland_vlad_victory_candidate = yes }
					TVA = { exists = yes ADISCORD_vorkerland_dorian_victory_candidate = yes }
				}
			}
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_begin_reunification = yes
		}
	}
}

# --- collapse_map_effects ---
ADISCORD_vorkerland_end_internal_wars = {
	# Central claimant wars.
	if = { limit = { country_exists = WRK country_exists = VAD } WRK = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = VAD } white_peace = VAD } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = WRK country_exists = TVA } WRK = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = TVA } white_peace = TVA } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = WRK country_exists = TGD } WRK = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = TGD } white_peace = TGD } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = WRK country_exists = EYR } WRK = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = EYR } white_peace = EYR } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = WRK country_exists = EGC } WRK = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = EGC } white_peace = EGC } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VAD country_exists = TVA } VAD = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = TVA } white_peace = TVA } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VAD country_exists = TGD } VAD = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = TGD } white_peace = TGD } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VAD country_exists = EYR } VAD = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = EYR } white_peace = EYR } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VAD country_exists = EGC } VAD = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = EGC } white_peace = EGC } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TVA country_exists = TGD } TVA = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = TGD } white_peace = TGD } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TVA country_exists = EYR } TVA = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = EYR } white_peace = EYR } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TVA country_exists = EGC } TVA = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = EGC } white_peace = EGC } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = WRK country_exists = RIV } WRK = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = RIV } white_peace = RIV } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = WRK country_exists = REV } WRK = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = REV } white_peace = REV } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = WRK country_exists = YOR } WRK = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = YOR } white_peace = YOR } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VAD country_exists = RIV } VAD = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = RIV } white_peace = RIV } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VAD country_exists = REV } VAD = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = REV } white_peace = REV } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VAD country_exists = YOR } VAD = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = YOR } white_peace = YOR } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TVA country_exists = RIV } TVA = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = RIV } white_peace = RIV } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TVA country_exists = REV } TVA = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = REV } white_peace = REV } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TVA country_exists = YOR } TVA = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = YOR } white_peace = YOR } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = WRK country_exists = NDN } WRK = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = NDN } white_peace = NDN } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = WRK country_exists = SWB } WRK = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = SWB } white_peace = SWB } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VAD country_exists = NDN } VAD = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = NDN } white_peace = NDN } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VAD country_exists = SWB } VAD = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = SWB } white_peace = SWB } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TVA country_exists = NDN } TVA = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = NDN } white_peace = NDN } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TVA country_exists = SWB } TVA = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = SWB } white_peace = SWB } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = WRK country_exists = VHV } WRK = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = VHV } white_peace = VHV } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = WRK country_exists = OSV } WRK = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = OSV } white_peace = OSV } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VAD country_exists = VHV } VAD = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = VHV } white_peace = VHV } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VAD country_exists = OSV } VAD = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = OSV } white_peace = OSV } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TVA country_exists = VHV } TVA = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = VHV } white_peace = VHV } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TVA country_exists = OSV } TVA = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = OSV } white_peace = OSV } } }

	# Local wars may still be active when a central claimant wins. Close them
	# explicitly before applying that claimant's final political map.
	if = { limit = { country_exists = ZAO country_exists = WPA } ZAO = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = WPA } white_peace = WPA } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = WPS country_exists = ZAO } WPS = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = ZAO } white_peace = ZAO } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = ZAO country_exists = PSD } ZAO = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = PSD } white_peace = PSD } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = ZAO country_exists = PWR } ZAO = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = PWR } white_peace = PWR } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = WPA country_exists = PSD } WPA = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = PSD } white_peace = PSD } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = WPA country_exists = PWR } WPA = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = PWR } white_peace = PWR } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = WPS country_exists = PSD } WPS = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = PSD } white_peace = PSD } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = WPS country_exists = PWR } WPS = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = PWR } white_peace = PWR } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VLA country_exists = EBA } VLA = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = EBA } white_peace = EBA } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = ROM country_exists = DVA } ROM = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = DVA } white_peace = DVA } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = SOL country_exists = SRA } SOL = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = SRA } white_peace = SRA } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TRU country_exists = ZTA } TRU = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = ZTA } white_peace = ZTA } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = ROM country_exists = VAD } ROM = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = VAD } white_peace = VAD } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TRU country_exists = VAD } TRU = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = VAD } white_peace = VAD } } }
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_clear_superevent_flags = {
	clr_global_flag = superevent_stelander_empire
	clr_global_flag = superevent_stelander_party_victory
	clr_global_flag = superevent_stelander_shabrat_victory
	clr_global_flag = superevent_vorkerland_civilwar
	clr_global_flag = superevent_vorkerland_dirty_opening
	clr_global_flag = superevent_vorkerland_worker_victory
	clr_global_flag = superevent_vorkerland_utilitarian_victory
	clr_global_flag = superevent_vorkerland_vlad_victory
	clr_global_flag = superevent_vorkerland_dorian_victory
}

# Unscoped playback. Observer/spectator has no human country, so a
# human-only dispatch never reaches the local client.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_play_superevent_sound = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = superevent_vorkerland_dirty_opening }
		sound_effect = superevent_vorkerland_dirty_opening_sound_e
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = superevent_vorkerland_utilitarian_victory }
		sound_effect = superevent_vorkerland_utilitarian_victory_sound_e
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = superevent_vorkerland_worker_victory }
		sound_effect = superevent_vorkerland_worker_victory_sound_e
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = superevent_vorkerland_vlad_victory }
		sound_effect = superevent_vorkerland_vlad_victory_sound_e
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = superevent_vorkerland_dorian_victory }
		sound_effect = superevent_vorkerland_dorian_victory_sound_e
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = superevent_stelander_party_victory }
		sound_effect = superevent_stelander_party_victory_sound_e
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = superevent_stelander_shabrat_victory }
		sound_effect = superevent_stelander_party_victory_sound_e
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = superevent_stelander_empire }
		sound_effect = superevent_stelander_empire_sound_e
	}
	else = {
		sound_effect = superevent_vorkerland_civilwar_sound_e
	}
	play_song = "one_minute_of_silence"
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = superevent_stelander_shabrat_victory }
		STS = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_ai = no }
				# The audio lead-in outlasts the presentation sound, independently of game speed or closing the window.
				scoped_play_song = "ADISCORD_stp_civil_war_end_after_superevent"
			}
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_play_collapse_superevent_audio = {
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_play_superevent_sound = yes
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_play_local_superevent_audio = {
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_play_superevent_sound = yes
}

# player_context scripted GUI refreshes when the hidden presentation event
# runs on a human country. An AI-only caller still fires it locally so
# observer mode keeps the unscoped audio path.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_show_dirty_opening_superevent = {
	if = {
		limit = { any_country = { is_ai = no } }
		random_country = {
			limit = { is_ai = no }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_superevent.4 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_superevent.4 }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_show_worker_victory_superevent = {
	set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_victory_announced
	if = {
		limit = { any_country = { is_ai = no } }
		random_country = {
			limit = { is_ai = no }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_superevent.2 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_superevent.2 }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_show_utilitarian_victory_superevent = {

	if = {
