ADISCORD_vorkerland_finalize_wrk_formation = {
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_worker
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_joint
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_utilitarian
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_inherit_integrated_claimant_cores = yes
	32 = { remove_core_of = WKR }
	33 = { remove_core_of = WKR }
	200 = { remove_core_of = WKR }
	201 = { remove_core_of = WKR }
	75 = { remove_core_of = VAD }
	106 = { remove_core_of = VAD }
	107 = { remove_core_of = VAD }
	121 = { remove_core_of = VAD }
	36 = { remove_core_of = TVA }
	37 = { remove_core_of = TVA }
	38 = { remove_core_of = TVA }
	39 = { remove_core_of = TVA }
	324 = { remove_core_of = TVA }
	set_capital = { state = 32 }
}

# The restored tag is the dormant original WRK country object. Normalize its
# historical crisis package for every claimant route before a winner is
# annexed into it; otherwise VAD/TVA victories can revive pre-collapse spirits.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_scrub_dormant_wrk_crisis = {
	remove_ideas = WRK_ashes_of_the_crown
	remove_ideas = WRK_hourglass_of_discord
	remove_ideas = WRK_constitution_of_the_republic
	remove_ideas = WRK_birthplace_of_the_first_revolution
	remove_ideas = WRK_birthplace_of_the_first_revolution_front_republic
	remove_ideas = WRK_birthplace_of_the_first_revolution_renewed_mandate
	remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_erased_nations
	remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_erased_nations_relief_1
	remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_erased_nations_relief_2
}

# Preserve the exact worker-route reform stage before WKR is annexed; "none"
# is meaningful because wartime guarantees may already have removed a burden.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_inherit_worker_reform_spirits = {
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_scrub_dormant_wrk_crisis = yes
	if = {
		limit = { WKR = { has_idea = WRK_birthplace_of_the_first_revolution_renewed_mandate } }
		add_ideas = WRK_birthplace_of_the_first_revolution_renewed_mandate
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { WKR = { has_idea = WRK_birthplace_of_the_first_revolution_front_republic } }
		add_ideas = WRK_birthplace_of_the_first_revolution_front_republic
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { WKR = { has_idea = WRK_birthplace_of_the_first_revolution } }
		add_ideas = WRK_birthplace_of_the_first_revolution
	}
	if = {
		limit = { WKR = { has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_erased_nations_relief_2 } }
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_erased_nations_relief_2
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { WKR = { has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_erased_nations_relief_1 } }
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_erased_nations_relief_1
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { WKR = { has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_erased_nations } }
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_erased_nations
	}
}
ADISCORD_vorkerland_form_wrk_from_wkr = {
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VAD } VAD = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_release_losing_claimant_subjects = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TVA } TVA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_release_losing_claimant_subjects = yes } }
	WKR = {
		every_other_country = {
			limit = { is_in_faction_with = PREV has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary }
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reunification_ally
		}
	}
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_prepare_claimants_for_formation = yes
	# These are claimant-war command structures, not postwar institutions.
	WKR = {
		remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_front_operations_bureau
		remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_republican_mission_commands
		remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_normative_campaign_tables
	}
	# SOL is an authored WKR protectorate, not cargo of annex_country. Detach it
	# before consuming WKR, then explicitly bind it to the new WRK object below.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_verified
			country_exists = SOL
		}
		SOL = {
			if = { limit = { is_subject = yes } overlord = { set_autonomy = { target = SOL autonomy_state = autonomy_free } } }
			if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
		}
	}
	# Materialize the dormant destination before moving characters or annexing
	# the winner. change_tag_from only follows the human player; it does not
	# replace the country object or transfer its armed forces.
	transfer_state = 32
	32 = { set_state_controller_to = WRK }
	set_capital = { state = 32 }
	inherit_technology = WKR
	load_focus_tree = {
		tree = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_focus
		keep_completed = yes
		copy_completed_from = WKR
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_safe_with_loyalists }
		WKR = {
			set_nationality = {
				target_country = WRK
				character = WRK_Nikita_Worcker
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		WKR = {
			set_nationality = {
				target_country = WRK
				character = WRK_Anton_Bagley
			}
		}
	}
	# These two stay on WRK through the war unless annex moved them to WKR.
	# Bring both copies back before WKR is consumed so elections can promote
	# an already recruited character.
	WKR = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = WRK_Mark_Yastrebtsev }
			set_nationality = { target_country = WRK character = WRK_Mark_Yastrebtsev }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = WRK_Necro_Filopo }
			set_nationality = { target_country = WRK character = WRK_Necro_Filopo }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { WKR = { is_ai = no } }
		change_tag_from = WKR
	}
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_inherit_worker_reform_spirits = yes
	WKR = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_refund_integrations_to_successor = yes }
	annex_country = { target = WKR transfer_troops = yes }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VAD } annex_country = { target = VAD transfer_troops = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TVA } annex_country = { target = TVA transfer_troops = yes } }
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_absorb_wtd_after_reunification = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_finalize_wrk_formation = yes
	set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_worker
	set_cosmetic_tag = WRK_vorkerland_emergency
	set_politics = { ruling_party = pragmatism elections_allowed = no }
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_safe_with_loyalists }
		promote_character = {
			character = WRK_Nikita_Worcker
			ideology = neo_vorkerism
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_cosmetic_tag = WRK_vorkerland_utilitarian_republic
		# The leader role must exist before the government flips to utilitarism;
		# otherwise the generated-leader fallback can win the race.
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_promote_anton_bagley = yes
		set_politics = { ruling_party = utilitarism elections_allowed = no }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_verified
			country_exists = SOL
		}
		SOL = {
			if = { limit = { is_subject = yes } overlord = { set_autonomy = { target = SOL autonomy_state = autonomy_free } } }
			if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
		}
		puppet = SOL
		set_autonomy = { target = SOL autonomy_state = autonomy_district_in_Vorkerland freedom_level = 0.10 }
		SOL = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_party_identity = yes
			set_cosmetic_tag = SOL_vorkerland_worker_protectorate
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_protectorate
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_rebound_to_wrk
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_vla_alliance_accepted country_exists = VLA }
		create_faction_from_template = { template = faction_template_ADISCORD_standard name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_free_republics_alliance }
		add_to_faction = VLA
	}
	focus_unlock = yes
	mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_reunification_allies = yes
}
ADISCORD_vorkerland_form_wrk_from_vad = {
	if = { limit = { country_exists = WKR } WKR = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_release_losing_claimant_subjects = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TVA } TVA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_release_losing_claimant_subjects = yes } }
	VAD = {
		every_other_country = {
			limit = { is_in_faction_with = PREV has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary }
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reunification_ally
		}
	}
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_prepare_claimants_for_formation = yes
	# The restoration cabinet and its dual-authority rival both end with the war.
	VAD = {
		remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_restoration_war_cabinet_2
		remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_dual_authority_protocol_2
	}
	transfer_state = 32
	32 = { set_state_controller_to = WRK }
	set_capital = { state = 32 }
	inherit_technology = VAD
	load_focus_tree = {
		tree = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_focus
		keep_completed = yes
		copy_completed_from = VAD
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_rescued_by_vlad }
		VAD = {
			set_nationality = {
				target_country = WRK
				character = WRK_VAD_Joint_Council
			}
			set_nationality = {
				target_country = WRK
				character = WRK_Nikita_Worcker
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_joint_government_formed } }
		VAD = {
			set_nationality = {
				target_country = WRK
				character = WRK_Vlad_Petrichev
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { VAD = { is_ai = no } }
		change_tag_from = VAD
	}
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_scrub_dormant_wrk_crisis = yes
	VAD = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_refund_integrations_to_successor = yes }
	annex_country = { target = VAD transfer_troops = yes }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = WKR } annex_country = { target = WKR transfer_troops = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TVA } annex_country = { target = TVA transfer_troops = yes } }
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_absorb_wtd_after_reunification = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_finalize_wrk_formation = yes
	# route_joint is the postwar focus route for the VAD claimant. Rescue and
	# this flag do not by themselves mean the rare Worker-Vlad council formed.
	set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_joint
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_joint_government_formed }
		set_cosmetic_tag = WRK_vorkerland_joint_government
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_appoint_joint_council = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_cosmetic_tag = VAD_vorkerland_restoration
		set_politics = { ruling_party = pragmatism elections_allowed = no }
		promote_character = {
			character = WRK_Vlad_Petrichev
			ideology = imperial_restorationism
		}
		set_country_leader_portrait = {
			ideology = pragmatism
			portrait = GFX_portrait_WRK_Vlad_Petrichev_civilwar
		}
	}
	# A loyalist SOL restored by force remains a protectorate of the reunited
	# state. A SOL that accepted Armi's invitation voluntarily remains sovereign.
	# Normalize both relations explicitly after VAD is consumed so the outcome
	# never depends on how annex_country treats the winner's former subjects.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_verified
			country_exists = SOL
		}
		SOL = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_subject = yes }
				overlord = { set_autonomy = { target = SOL autonomy_state = autonomy_free } }
			}
			if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
		}
		puppet = SOL
		set_autonomy = { target = SOL autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet freedom_level = 0.10 }
		SOL = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_party_identity = yes }
		create_faction_from_template = { template = faction_template_ADISCORD_standard name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_restoration_alliance }
		add_to_faction = SOL
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_sol_alliance_accepted
			country_exists = SOL
		}
		SOL = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_subject = yes }
				overlord = { set_autonomy = { target = SOL autonomy_state = autonomy_free } }
			}
			if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
		}
		create_faction_from_template = { template = faction_template_ADISCORD_standard name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_restoration_alliance }
		add_to_faction = SOL
	}
	focus_unlock = yes
	mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_reunification_allies = yes
}
ADISCORD_vorkerland_form_wrk_from_tva = {
	if = { limit = { country_exists = WKR } WKR = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_release_losing_claimant_subjects = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VAD } VAD = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_release_losing_claimant_subjects = yes } }
	TVA = {
		every_other_country = {
			limit = { is_in_faction_with = PREV has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary }
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reunification_ally
		}
	}
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_prepare_claimants_for_formation = yes
	transfer_state = 32
	32 = { set_state_controller_to = WRK }
	set_capital = { state = 32 }
	inherit_technology = TVA
	load_focus_tree = {
		tree = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_focus
		keep_completed = yes
		copy_completed_from = TVA
	}
	TVA = {
		set_nationality = {
			target_country = WRK
			character = TVA_Dorian_Worx
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { TVA = { is_ai = no } }
		change_tag_from = TVA
	}
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_scrub_dormant_wrk_crisis = yes
	TVA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_refund_integrations_to_successor = yes }
	annex_country = { target = TVA transfer_troops = yes }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = WKR } annex_country = { target = WKR transfer_troops = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VAD } annex_country = { target = VAD transfer_troops = yes } }
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_absorb_wtd_after_reunification = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_finalize_wrk_formation = yes
	# route_utilitarian is retained as the established focus/save contract for
	# the TVA claimant. Dorian's visible state and politics remain technocratic.
	set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_utilitarian
	drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
	set_cosmetic_tag = WRK_vorkerland_technocracy
	set_politics = { ruling_party = technocracy elections_allowed = no }
	promote_character = {
		character = TVA_Dorian_Worx
		ideology = technocracy_ideology
	}
	# Level three is the reward for Doctor Worx's verified central victory; the
	# wartime capstone itself remains bounded at level two.
	remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_field_directorate
	remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_field_directorate_2
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_field_directorate_3 } }
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_field_directorate_3
	}
	# TGD is already the successful Firnov-Tekhlar protectorate. Rebind it to
	# the terminal WRK country explicitly because annexing TVA need not transfer
	# the engine's subject cache.
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = TGD
			TGD = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_joined_worx_directorate }
		}
		TGD = {
			if = { limit = { is_subject = yes } overlord = { set_autonomy = { target = TGD autonomy_state = autonomy_free } } }
			if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
		}
		puppet = TGD
		set_autonomy = { target = TGD autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet freedom_level = 0.10 }
	}
	# Oitfort's wartime protectorate ends with reunification. State 34 was
	# already consumed by ADISCORD_vorkerland_absorb_wtd_after_reunification.
	focus_unlock = yes
	mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_reunification_allies = yes
}

# Nikita's verified central victory is the only route that may reopen the
# civilian succession. Anton, Vlad and Worx keep their wartime cabinets.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_offer_worker_mandate = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WRK
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_worker
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_safe_with_loyalists
			has_country_leader = { character = WRK_Nikita_Worcker ruling_only = yes }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_mandate_offered }
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_mandate_offered
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.100 days = 2 }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_wrk_schedule_elections = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_mandate_resolved } }
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_mandate_resolved
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_elections_scheduled
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.101 days = 14 }
	}
}

# The result event runs this in immediate so the new leader is already in
# office when the description is shown.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_wrk_resolve_elections = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_elections_scheduled
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_election_settled }
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_election_settled
		random_list = {
			1 = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_wrk_elect_worcker = yes }
			1 = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_wrk_elect_yastrebtsev = yes }
			1 = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_wrk_elect_filopo = yes }
		}
		news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_news.3 hours = 1 }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_wrk_usurp_mandate = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_mandate_resolved } }
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_mandate_resolved
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_usurped
		}
		set_politics = { ruling_party = pragmatism elections_allowed = no }
		promote_character = {
			character = WRK_Nikita_Worcker
			ideology = neo_vorkerism
		}
		# Bind the victory portrait to the character so a later ideology-slot
		# refresh cannot restore the wartime face.
		set_portraits = {
			character = WRK_Nikita_Worcker
			civilian = { large = GFX_portrait_WRK_Nikita_Worcker_victory }
		}
		set_country_leader_portrait = {
			ideology = pragmatism
			portrait = GFX_portrait_WRK_Nikita_Worcker_victory
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_wrk_elect_worcker = {
	set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_elected
	set_politics = { ruling_party = pragmatism elections_allowed = yes }
	promote_character = {
		character = WRK_Nikita_Worcker
		ideology = neo_vorkerism
	}
	set_popularities = {
		pragmatism = 48
		humanism = 22
		chauvinism = 18
		technocracy = 12
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_wrk_elect_yastrebtsev = {
	set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_yastrebtsev_elected
	set_politics = { ruling_party = humanism elections_allowed = yes }
	promote_character = {
		character = WRK_Mark_Yastrebtsev
		ideology = humanism_ideology
	}
	set_popularities = {
		humanism = 46
		pragmatism = 28
		chauvinism = 14
		technocracy = 12
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_wrk_elect_filopo = {
	set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_filopo_elected
	set_politics = { ruling_party = chauvinism elections_allowed = yes }
	promote_character = {
		character = WRK_Necro_Filopo
		ideology = chauvinism_ideology
	}
	set_popularities = {
		chauvinism = 46
		pragmatism = 28
		humanism = 14
		technocracy = 12
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_verify_reunified_wrk = {
	if = {
		limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_reunification_verified = yes }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reunification_verified
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_finalize_reunified_wrk = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_set_phase_postwar_integration = yes
	}
}

# Run only after the restored WRK and its mutually-exclusive route have passed
# their next-day postcondition. This is the sole new-controller terminal commit:
# it records the winner, shuts down wartime producers and leaves legacy outcome
# effects safe to call before this point.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_finalize_reunified_wrk = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WRK
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_reunification_verified = yes
		}
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_won
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vlad_won
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dorian_won
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_unifier
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_worker }
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_won
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_victory_announced } }
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_safe_with_loyalists } }
					ADISCORD_vorkerland_show_utilitarian_victory_superevent = yes
				}
				else = {
					ADISCORD_vorkerland_show_worker_victory_superevent = yes
				}
			}
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_resolve_unguaranteed_free_republics = yes
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_offer_worker_mandate = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_joint }
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vlad_won
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_victory_announced } }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_show_vlad_victory_superevent = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_utilitarian }
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dorian_won
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_victory_announced } }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_show_dorian_victory_superevent = yes
			}
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_war_finished
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_finished
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_reconcile_campaign_state = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_retire_civil_war_modifiers = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_refresh_vorkerland_terminal_assistance = yes
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_outcome_pending
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_preparation_expired
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_showdown_requested
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_queue_initialized
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_launch_round_started
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_war_launch_scheduled
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_war_launch_final_retry
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_consolidation_check_scheduled
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reunification_retry
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reunification_failed
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_finish_civil_war_exhaustion = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_split_external_gate = yes
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = WTD }
			WTD = {
				clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wtd_join_retry
				clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wtd_join_failed
				clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wtd_fighting_for_worx
			}
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_reunification_allies = {
	if = {
		limit = { any_other_country = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reunification_ally } }
		if = { limit = { is_in_faction = no } create_faction_from_template = { template = faction_template_ADISCORD_standard name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_free_republics_alliance } }
		every_other_country = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reunification_ally }
			if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes NOT = { is_in_faction_with = PREV } } leave_faction = yes }
			WRK = { add_to_faction = PREV }
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reunification_ally
		}
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_mandatory_major }
		set_major = no
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_mandatory_major
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary_retry_used
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary_failed
	}
}



ADISCORD_vorkerland_refund_integrations_to_successor = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_eyr_district_paid } clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_eyr_district_paid WRK = { add_political_power = 15 } remove_decision = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_eyr_district }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_egc_district_paid } clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_egc_district_paid WRK = { add_political_power = 15 } remove_decision = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_egc_district }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_riv_district_paid } clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_riv_district_paid WRK = { add_political_power = 15 } remove_decision = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_riv_district }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_rev_district_paid } clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_rev_district_paid WRK = { add_political_power = 15 } remove_decision = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_rev_district }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_yor_district_paid } clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_yor_district_paid WRK = { add_political_power = 15 } remove_decision = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_yor_district }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_ndn_district_paid } clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_ndn_district_paid WRK = { add_political_power = 10 } remove_decision = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_ndn_district }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_swb_district_paid } clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_swb_district_paid WRK = { add_political_power = 10 } remove_decision = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_swb_district }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_vhv_district_paid } clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_vhv_district_paid WRK = { add_political_power = 15 } remove_decision = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_vhv_district }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_osv_district_paid } clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_osv_district_paid WRK = { add_political_power = 15 } remove_decision = ADISCORD_vorkerland_integrate_osv_district }
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_reconcile_coalition_obligation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_mandatory_major
			OR = {
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_war_finished
				is_in_faction = no
				AND = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_started has_war = no }
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary_failed
			}
		}
		set_major = no
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_mandatory_major
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary_retry_used
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary_failed
	}
}

# POSTWAR IMPERIAL RECLAMATION
# COUNTRY WRK: called after the decision checks its unlock, cost and peace.
# Recheck every candidate at execution. The exclusive chain starts exactly
# one war and preserves the authored priority; do not replace it with a loop.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_continue_imperial_reunification = {
	if = {
		limit = { EYR = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_imperial_reclamation_target = yes } }
		declare_war_on = { target = EYR type = annex_everything }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { EGC = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_imperial_reclamation_target = yes } }
		declare_war_on = { target = EGC type = annex_everything }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { RIV = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_imperial_reclamation_target = yes } }
		declare_war_on = { target = RIV type = annex_everything }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { REV = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_imperial_reclamation_target = yes } }
		declare_war_on = { target = REV type = annex_everything }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { YOR = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_imperial_reclamation_target = yes } }
		declare_war_on = { target = YOR type = annex_everything }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NDN = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_imperial_reclamation_target = yes } }
		declare_war_on = { target = NDN type = annex_everything }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { SWB = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_imperial_reclamation_target = yes } }
		declare_war_on = { target = SWB type = annex_everything }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { VHV = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_imperial_reclamation_target = yes } }
		declare_war_on = { target = VHV type = annex_everything }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { OSV = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_imperial_reclamation_target = yes } }
		declare_war_on = { target = OSV type = annex_everything }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { ZAO = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_imperial_reclamation_target = yes } }
		declare_war_on = { target = ZAO type = annex_everything }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PWR = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_imperial_reclamation_target = yes } }
		declare_war_on = { target = PWR type = annex_everything }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { VLA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_imperial_reclamation_target = yes } }
		declare_war_on = { target = VLA type = annex_everything }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { ROM = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_imperial_reclamation_target = yes } }
		declare_war_on = { target = ROM type = annex_everything }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { TRU = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_imperial_reclamation_target = yes } }
		declare_war_on = { target = TRU type = annex_everything }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { WPA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_imperial_reclamation_target = yes } }
		declare_war_on = { target = WPA type = annex_everything }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { WPS = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_imperial_reclamation_target = yes } }
		declare_war_on = { target = WPS type = annex_everything }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PSD = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_imperial_reclamation_target = yes } }
		declare_war_on = { target = PSD type = annex_everything }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { EBA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_imperial_reclamation_target = yes } }
		declare_war_on = { target = EBA type = annex_everything }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { DVA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_imperial_reclamation_target = yes } }
		declare_war_on = { target = DVA type = annex_everything }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { SRA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_imperial_reclamation_target = yes } }
		declare_war_on = { target = SRA type = annex_everything }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { ZTA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_imperial_reclamation_target = yes } }
		declare_war_on = { target = ZTA type = annex_everything }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { CSL = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_imperial_reclamation_target = yes } }
		declare_war_on = { target = CSL type = annex_everything }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { IBA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_imperial_reclamation_target = yes } }
		declare_war_on = { target = IBA type = annex_everything }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { IBL = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_imperial_reclamation_target = yes } }
		declare_war_on = { target = IBL type = annex_everything }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { TGD = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_imperial_reclamation_target = yes } }
		declare_war_on = { target = TGD type = annex_everything }
	}
}

# --- release_effects ---
# Intercept the dormant WRK shell in the country scope supplied by native
# release on-actions. FROM remains the releasing country in all three hooks.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_intercept_premature_wrk_release = {
	if = {
		limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_premature_wrk = yes }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_premature_wrk_release_intercepted_v1
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_main_claimant = yes
					exists = yes
					is_subject = no
					NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
				}
			}
			FROM = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_dissolve_premature_wrk_as_claimant = yes }
		}
		else = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_premature_wrk_release_to_living_claimant = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_premature_wrk_subject_cleanup_active }
			FROM = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_premature_wrk = yes }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_premature_wrk_release_intercepted_v1
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_premature_wrk_release_to_living_claimant = yes
	}
}

# --- rom_tru_effects ---
ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_initialize_war_content = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_war_content_initialized } }
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_war_content_initialized
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_desert_eagle_staff } }
			add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_desert_eagle_staff
		}
		set_popularities = {
			etatism = 45
			pragmatism = 30
			humanism = 15
			chauvinism = 5
			utilitarism = 5
		}
		# The shared collapse setup already grants 10,000 men, 1,800 rifles and
		# 140 support sets. These finite deltas bring ROM to the approved total.
		add_manpower = 3000
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 400 producer = ROM }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 20 producer = ROM }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment_1 amount = 48 producer = ROM }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_tru_initialize_war_content = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tru_war_content_initialized } }
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tru_war_content_initialized
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tru_emergency_river_council } }
			add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tru_emergency_river_council
		}
		set_popularities = {
			chauvinism = 42
			pragmatism = 25
			humanism = 18
			etatism = 8
			utilitarism = 5
			technocracy = 2
		}
		# The shared collapse setup already grants 11,000 men, 2,000 rifles and
		# 160 support sets. These finite deltas bring TRU to the approved total.
		add_manpower = 3000
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 400 producer = TRU }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 24 producer = TRU }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment_1 amount = 48 producer = TRU }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_assemble_valley_columns = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_valley_columns_raised } }
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_valley_columns_raised
		add_manpower = 2500
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 720 producer = ROM }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 36 producer = ROM }
		PREV = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_ensure_line_infantry_brigade_template = yes }
		capital_scope = {
			create_unit = {
				division = "division_template = \"Line Infantry Brigade\" start_experience_factor = 0.15 start_equipment_factor = 0.70"
				owner = PREV
			}
		}
		add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_valley_columns days = 90 }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_tru_arm_river_battalions = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tru_river_battalions_raised } }
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tru_river_battalions_raised
		add_manpower = 3000
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 720 producer = TRU }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 48 producer = TRU }
		PREV = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_ensure_line_infantry_brigade_template = yes }
		capital_scope = {
			create_unit = {
				division = "division_template = \"Line Infantry Brigade\" start_experience_factor = 0.15 start_equipment_factor = 0.70"
				owner = PREV
			}
		}
		add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tru_crossing_columns days = 90 }
	}
}

# The first local victor leads the league. Membership does not call the other
# republic into its wars; both must be independent and outside existing blocs.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_form_free_republics_league = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = { tag = ROM owns_state = 144 owns_state = 145 owns_state = 319 owns_state = 321 NOT = { has_war_with = DVA } }
				AND = { tag = TRU owns_state = 199 NOT = { has_war_with = ZTA } }
			}
			ROM = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no has_capitulated = no }
			TRU = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no has_capitulated = no }
			NOT = { ROM = { has_war_with = TRU } }
		}
		create_faction_from_template = {
			template = faction_template_ADISCORD_standard
			name = faction_vorkerland_free_republics_league
		}
		if = { limit = { tag = ROM } add_to_faction = TRU }
		else = { add_to_faction = ROM }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_valley_offensive_success = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_valley_offensive_resolved } }
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_valley_offensive_resolved
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_valley_offensive_won
		add_political_power = 40
		add_stability = 0.05
	}
}

		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_valley_offensive_resolved } }

		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_valley_offensive_timed_out
		add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_stalled_offensive days = 60 }
	}
}

# --- stalemate_effects ---
# on_war_relation_added calls these schedulers only for the attacking side.
# Permanent schedule/resolution guards make each window one-shot per country.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_schedule_central_stalemate_deadline = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_stalemate_campaign_active = yes
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_main_claimant = yes
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_has_live_central_minor_war = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_stalemate_deadline_scheduled }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_stalemate_deadline_resolved }
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_stalemate_deadline_scheduled
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_stalemate.1 days = 240 }
	}
}

# The regional declarations precede the central-preparation flag by a day, so
# scheduling uses the collapse-war gate. The deadline itself requires the full
# phase trigger before it can grant anything.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_schedule_solarino_stalemate_deadline = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_started
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_wars_started
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_finished }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_has_live_solarino_war = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solarino_stalemate_deadline_scheduled }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solarino_stalemate_deadline_resolved }
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solarino_stalemate_deadline_scheduled
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_stalemate.2 days = 300 }
	}
}

# Resolution flags are written before the payload. A dead or completed front
# consumes its deadline without granting a buff and cannot be re-armed later.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_resolve_central_stalemate_deadline = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_main_claimant = yes
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_stalemate_deadline_scheduled
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_stalemate_deadline_resolved }
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_stalemate_deadline_resolved
		if = {
			limit = {
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_stalemate_campaign_active = yes
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_has_live_central_minor_war = yes
			}
			set_country_flag = { flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_breakthrough_window_active days = 75 }
			add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ai_breakthrough_window days = 75 }
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_resolve_solarino_stalemate_deadline = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = SOL
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solarino_stalemate_deadline_scheduled
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solarino_stalemate_deadline_resolved }
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solarino_stalemate_deadline_resolved
		if = {
			limit = {
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_stalemate_campaign_active = yes
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_has_live_solarino_war = yes
			}
			set_country_flag = { flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solarino_breakthrough_window_active days = 75 }
			add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ai_breakthrough_window days = 75 }
		}
	}
}

# --- story_effects ---
# Compact story layer for the background Vorkerland civil war. These effects
# may change only narrative flags and small country resources. They do not own
# wars, peace, state transfer, annexation, subjects, or phase progression.

ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_announce_verified_showdown = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_started
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_edges_verified = yes
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_required = yes
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_launch_failed }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_showdown_announced }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_showdown_announced
		news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.1 }
	}
}

# The collapse roll decides what happened to Nikita Worker and records it in one
# of four global flags. That roll lives in the collapse layer, which owns no
# presentation, so the world would otherwise never learn the single most
# consequential fact of the whole scenario. Call this once from the collapse
# opening, after the fate flags are set.
#
# The guard is released again when no fate flag is present so that a call placed
# too early cannot permanently silence the report.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_report_worker_fate = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_worker_fate_reported } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_worker_fate_reported
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_safe_with_loyalists }
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_worker_survival_reported
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.10 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_rescued_by_vlad }
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_worker_rescue_reported
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.11 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_missing }
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_worker_disappearance_reported
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.12 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_killed }
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_worker_death_reported
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.13 }
		}
		else = {
			clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_worker_fate_reported
		}
	}
}

# Presentation for the twelve iconic objectives. The phase layer resolves the
# capture and writes global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_objective_last; this reads that
# id, records it so the same centre can never be reported twice, and fires the
# single objective news event.
#
# Called from ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_check_first_claimant_capital_fall, which
# on_state_control_changed already invokes after the twelve objective checks. It
# therefore observes the completed result of the whole state change rather than
# a half-applied one, and one control change can never queue several objective
# reports that would all render with the last id.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_report_iconic_objective = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_objective_last value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_objective_last value = 12 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			NOT = {
				is_in_array = {
					array = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_objectives_reported
					value = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_objective_last
				}
			}
			# The three claimant capitals already carry a dedicated first-fall
			# report. Without these the same city would be announced twice on the
			# same day by two different events.
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					check_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_objective_last value = 1 compare = equals }
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_wkr_capital_fell_first
				}
			}
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					check_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_objective_last value = 2 compare = equals }
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_vad_capital_fell_first
				}
			}
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					check_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_objective_last value = 3 compare = equals }
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_tva_capital_fell_first
				}
			}
		}
		# A global array is the ordinary array argument with a global. prefix.
		# The script API defines no separate global-array effect or trigger, and
		# a mistyped one is accepted by the parser and then does nothing.
		add_to_array = {
			array = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_objectives_reported
			value = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_objective_last
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_objective_last value = 1 compare = equals } }
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.50 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_objective_last value = 2 compare = equals } }
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.51 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_objective_last value = 3 compare = equals } }
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.52 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_objective_last value = 4 compare = equals } }
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.53 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_objective_last value = 5 compare = equals } }
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.54 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_objective_last value = 6 compare = equals } }
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.55 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_objective_last value = 7 compare = equals } }
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.56 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_objective_last value = 8 compare = equals } }
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.57 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_objective_last value = 9 compare = equals } }
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.58 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_objective_last value = 10 compare = equals } }
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.59 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_objective_last value = 11 compare = equals } }
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.60 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_objective_last value = 12 compare = equals } }
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.61 }
		}
	}
}

# Presentation for the six mutually exclusive doctrine branches. Called in the
# declaring claimant's own scope from the focus completion reward that sets the
# variant flag; the per-branch global flag makes a repeated or duplicated call
# harmless. Each branch fires its own news event: a shared major news item
# with check_variable title/desc blocks painted the generic fallback.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_report_variant_declaration = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_variant_neo_vorkerist
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_variant_wkr_neo_vorkerist_reported }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_variant_wkr_neo_vorkerist_reported
		news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.31 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_variant_utilitarian
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_variant_wkr_utilitarian_reported }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_variant_wkr_utilitarian_reported
		news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.32 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_variant_imperial
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_variant_vad_imperial_reported }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_variant_vad_imperial_reported
		news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.33 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_variant_joint
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_variant_vad_joint_reported }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_variant_vad_joint_reported
		news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.34 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_variant_throughput
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_variant_tva_throughput_reported }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_variant_tva_throughput_reported
		news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.35 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_variant_preservation
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_variant_tva_preservation_reported }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_variant_tva_preservation_reported
		news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.36 }
	}
}

# A main claimant leaving the central war is a world event, not only a prompt for
# the victor. Each of the three is reported at most once, in its own scope, so
# the news names the side that fell.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_report_claimant_capitulation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_started
			tag = WKR
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_wkr_capitulation_reported }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_wkr_capitulation_reported
		news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.41 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_started
			tag = VAD
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_vad_capitulation_reported }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_vad_capitulation_reported
		news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.42 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_started
			tag = TVA
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_tva_capitulation_reported }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_tva_capitulation_reported
		news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.43 }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_reward_capital_winner = {
	add_war_support = 0.02
	add_command_power = 8
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_dispatch_wkr_capital_fall = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = WTD TVA = { exists = yes } }
		TVA = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_reward_capital_winner = yes
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.2 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { tag = VLA WKR = { exists = yes } }
		WKR = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_reward_capital_winner = yes
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.2 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { tag = SOL VAD = { exists = yes } }
		VAD = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_reward_capital_winner = yes
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.2 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_subject = yes }
		OVERLORD = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_reward_capital_winner = yes
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.2 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_reward_capital_winner = yes
		news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.2 }
	}
}

}

# ROOT is the new controller and FROM is the old controller in
# on_state_control_changed. Requiring the changed controller to hold the exact
# capital prevents an unrelated state change from naming the wrong victor.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_check_first_claimant_capital_fall = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_started
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_edges_verified = yes
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_launch_failed }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_first_claimant_capital_fell }
			FROM = { tag = WKR }
			32 = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			NOT = { tag = WKR }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_first_claimant_capital_fell
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_wkr_capital_fell_first
		WKR = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes }
				add_war_support = -0.02
				add_command_power = -5
			}
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_dispatch_wkr_capital_fall = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_started
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_edges_verified = yes
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_launch_failed }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_first_claimant_capital_fell }
			FROM = { tag = VAD }
			75 = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			NOT = { tag = VAD }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_first_claimant_capital_fell
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_vad_capital_fell_first
		VAD = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes }
				add_war_support = -0.02
				add_command_power = -5
			}
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_dispatch_vad_capital_fall = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_started
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_edges_verified = yes
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_launch_failed }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_first_claimant_capital_fell }
			FROM = { tag = TVA }
			36 = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			NOT = { tag = TVA }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_first_claimant_capital_fell
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_tva_capital_fell_first
		TVA = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes }
				add_war_support = -0.02
				add_command_power = -5
			}
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_dispatch_tva_capital_fall = yes
	}
	# Runs after the capital chain above so the iconic-objective report can see
	# which capital, if any, has just been announced separately. This effect is
	# the story layer's single on_state_control_changed entry point, so the
	# objective news needs no additional hook outside the story files.
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_report_iconic_objective = yes
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_offer_first_claimant_command_choice = {
	# on_capitulation calls this inside FROM, the winner, so the report has to be
	# scoped back to ROOT. Otherwise the news would name the side that won as the
	# side that fell.
	ROOT = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_report_claimant_capitulation = yes }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_started
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_war_finished }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reunification_verified }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_first_claimant_capitulated }
			NOT = { tag = WRK }
			ROOT = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_main_claimant = yes }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_first_claimant_capitulated
		if = {
			limit = { tag = WTD TVA = { exists = yes } }
			TVA = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.5 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { tag = VLA WKR = { exists = yes } }
			WKR = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.5 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { tag = SOL VAD = { exists = yes } }
			VAD = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.5 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { is_subject = yes }
			OVERLORD = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.5 } }
		}
		else = {
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.5 }
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_offer_post_reunification = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WRK
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reunification_verified
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_postwar_event_offered }
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_worker
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_joint
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_utilitarian
			}
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_postwar_event_offered
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.6 }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_finish_reunification = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WRK
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_postwar_choice_resolved }
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_postwar_choice_resolved
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_worker }
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_worker_finale_reported
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.7 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_joint }
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_joint_finale_reported
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.8 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_utilitarian }
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_worx_finale_reported
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.9 }
		}
	}
}

# --- war_economy_effects ---
# Claimant factory scaling is the only recurring civil-war producer.
# Called from the tag-specific monthly hooks for WKR, VAD and TVA.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_war_economy_dynamic_state = {
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_factory_count value = num_of_factories }

	# Capture what this claimant started with on the first tick after it is
	# created, then measure every later month against that. Re-captured while
	# it is still below one so a claimant that somehow spawns with no industry
	# does not divide by zero.
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_baseline
				value = 1
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_baseline
			value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_factory_count
		}
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_baseline min = 1 max = 1000 }

	set_variable = {
		var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_scale
		value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_factory_count
	}
	divide_variable = {
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_scale = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_baseline
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_scale min = 0.25 max = 1 }

	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_stance value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_wkr_strip_the_civil_economy
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_vad_levy_the_crown_estates
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_tva_run_the_grid_at_war_load
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_stance value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_wkr_keep_the_bread_ration
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_vad_protect_the_district_markets
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_tva_hold_back_civil_power
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_stance value = -1 }
	}

	# Stance weighted by retained industry. Gain axes use this, cost axes use
	# the bare stance.
	set_variable = {
		var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_bite
		value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_stance
	}
	multiply_variable = {
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_bite = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_scale
	}

	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_output value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_efficiency value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_manpower value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_mobilization value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_research value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_unrest value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_goods value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_authority value = 0 }

	# The councils argue about the shop floor: output against the ration.
	if = {
		limit = { tag = WKR }
		set_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_output
			value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_bite
		}
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_output = 0.10 }
		set_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_goods
			value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_stance
		}
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_goods = -0.05 }
		set_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_unrest
			value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_stance
		}
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_unrest = -0.05 }
		set_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_authority
			value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_stance
		}
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_authority = -0.10 }
	}

	# The chancery argues about the warrants: men and haste against consent.
	if = {
		limit = { tag = VAD }
		set_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_manpower
			value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_bite
		}
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_manpower = 0.015 }
		set_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_mobilization
			value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_bite
		}
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_mobilization = 0.15 }
		set_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_unrest
			value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_stance
		}
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_unrest = -0.04 }
		set_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_authority
			value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_stance
		}
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_authority = -0.15 }
	}

	# The load board argues about the current: the front or the laboratories.
	if = {
		limit = { tag = TVA }
		set_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_output
			value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_bite
		}
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_output = 0.12 }
		set_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_efficiency
			value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_bite
		}
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_efficiency = 0.05 }
		set_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_research
			value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_stance
		}
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_research = -0.05 }
		set_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_unrest
			value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_stance
		}
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_unrest = -0.06 }
		set_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_goods
			value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_stance
		}
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_goods = -0.06 }
	}

	# Percentage of the original industrial base still in hand, for the tooltip.
	set_variable = {
		var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_scale_display
		value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_scale
	}
	multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_scale_display = 100 }
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

	180 = { add_core_of = RUS add_claim_by = RUS }
	181 = { add_core_of = RUS add_claim_by = RUS }
	182 = { add_core_of = RUS add_claim_by = RUS }
	183 = { add_core_of = RUS add_claim_by = RUS }
	193 = { add_core_of = RUS add_claim_by = RUS }
	206 = { add_core_of = RUS add_claim_by = RUS }
	207 = { add_core_of = RUS add_claim_by = RUS }
	330 = { add_core_of = RUS }
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_core_dirty_sca = {
	165 = { add_core_of = RUS add_claim_by = RUS }
	166 = { add_core_of = RUS add_claim_by = RUS }
	172 = { add_core_of = RUS add_claim_by = RUS }

	211 = { add_core_of = RUS add_claim_by = RUS }
	212 = { add_core_of = RUS add_claim_by = RUS }
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_annex_dirty_target = {
	# annex_country can leave the tag alive on impassable leftovers.
	# Sweep remaining owned states so the neighbour does not survive the peace.
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_campaign_target value = 1 compare = equals } country_exists = SLA }
		annex_country = { target = SLA transfer_troops = no }
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = SLA }
			SLA = { every_owned_state = { set_state_owner_to = RUS set_state_controller_to = RUS } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_campaign_target value = 2 compare = equals } country_exists = RZA }
		annex_country = { target = RZA transfer_troops = no }
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = RZA }
			RZA = { every_owned_state = { set_state_owner_to = RUS set_state_controller_to = RUS } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_campaign_target value = 3 compare = equals } country_exists = MLR }
		annex_country = { target = MLR transfer_troops = no }
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = MLR }
			MLR = { every_owned_state = { set_state_owner_to = RUS set_state_controller_to = RUS } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_campaign_target value = 4 compare = equals } country_exists = ERT }
		# Resolve the separately occupied VAL claim before consuming ERT ownership.
		VAL = { if = { limit = { VAL_frontier_eastern_claim_ready = yes } VAL_frontier_settle_victory = yes } }
		annex_country = { target = ERT transfer_troops = no }
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = ERT }
			ERT = { every_owned_state = { set_state_owner_to = RUS set_state_controller_to = RUS } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_campaign_target value = 5 compare = equals } country_exists = IRT }
		annex_country = { target = IRT transfer_troops = no }
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = IRT }
			IRT = { every_owned_state = { set_state_owner_to = RUS set_state_controller_to = RUS } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_campaign_target value = 6 compare = equals } country_exists = SCA }
		annex_country = { target = SCA transfer_troops = no }
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = SCA }
			SCA = { every_owned_state = { set_state_owner_to = RUS set_state_controller_to = RUS } }
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_abort_dirty_campaign = {
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_dirty_campaign_active
	clear_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_campaign_target
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_start_dirty_campaign = {
	if = {
		limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_dirty_campaign_idle = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_campaign_target value = 1 compare = equals }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_borders_dirty_sla = yes
			}
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_dirty_campaign_active
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_core_dirty_sla = yes
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = SLA } }
				declare_war_on = { target = SLA type = annex_everything }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_campaign_target value = 2 compare = equals }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_borders_dirty_rza = yes
			}
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_dirty_campaign_active
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_core_dirty_rza = yes
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = RZA } }
				declare_war_on = { target = RZA type = annex_everything }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_campaign_target value = 3 compare = equals }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_borders_dirty_mlr = yes
			}
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_dirty_campaign_active
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_core_dirty_mlr = yes
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = MLR } }
				declare_war_on = { target = MLR type = annex_everything }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_campaign_target value = 4 compare = equals }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_borders_dirty_ert = yes
			}
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_dirty_campaign_active
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_core_dirty_ert = yes
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = ERT } }
				declare_war_on = { target = ERT type = annex_everything }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_campaign_target value = 5 compare = equals }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_borders_dirty_irt = yes
			}
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_dirty_campaign_active
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_core_dirty_irt = yes
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = IRT } }
				declare_war_on = { target = IRT type = annex_everything }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_campaign_target value = 6 compare = equals }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_borders_dirty_sca = yes
			}
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_dirty_campaign_active
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_core_dirty_sca = yes
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = SCA } }
				declare_war_on = { target = SCA type = annex_everything }
			}
		}
		else = {
			clear_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_campaign_target
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_release_lost_absorptions = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_sla_absorbed
			NOT = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_holds_sla_playable = yes }
			NOT = { has_war_with = SLA }
		}
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_sla_absorbed
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_rza_absorbed
			NOT = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_holds_rza_playable = yes }
			NOT = { has_war_with = RZA }
		}
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_rza_absorbed
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_mlr_absorbed
			NOT = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_holds_mlr_playable = yes }
			NOT = { has_war_with = MLR }
		}
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_mlr_absorbed
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_ert_absorbed
			NOT = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_holds_ert_playable = yes }
			NOT = { has_war_with = ERT }
		}
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_ert_absorbed
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_irt_absorbed
			NOT = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_holds_irt_playable = yes }
			NOT = { has_war_with = IRT }
		}
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_irt_absorbed
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_sca_absorbed
			NOT = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_holds_sca_playable = yes }
			NOT = { has_war_with = SCA }
		}
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_sca_absorbed
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_settle_dirty_target = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_campaign_target value = 1 compare = equals }
			OR = {
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_holds_sla_playable = yes
				AND = { country_exists = SLA has_war_with = SLA SLA = { has_capitulated = yes capital_scope = { OR = { is_controlled_by = RUS controller = { is_subject_of = RUS has_war_with = SLA } } } } }
			}
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_annex_dirty_target = yes
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_sla_absorbed
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_sla_counted } }
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_sla_counted
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_dirty_neighbors_taken value = 1 }
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_core_dirty_sla = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_close_khan_border_war_flags = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_abort_dirty_campaign = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_campaign_target value = 2 compare = equals }
			OR = {
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_holds_rza_playable = yes
				AND = { country_exists = RZA has_war_with = RZA RZA = { has_capitulated = yes capital_scope = { OR = { is_controlled_by = RUS controller = { is_subject_of = RUS has_war_with = RZA } } } } }
			}
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_annex_dirty_target = yes
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_rza_absorbed
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_rza_counted } }
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_rza_counted
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_dirty_neighbors_taken value = 1 }
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_core_dirty_rza = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_abort_dirty_campaign = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_campaign_target value = 3 compare = equals }
			OR = {
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_holds_mlr_playable = yes
				AND = { country_exists = MLR has_war_with = MLR MLR = { has_capitulated = yes capital_scope = { OR = { is_controlled_by = RUS controller = { is_subject_of = RUS has_war_with = MLR } } } } }
			}
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_annex_dirty_target = yes
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_mlr_absorbed
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_mlr_counted } }
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_mlr_counted
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_dirty_neighbors_taken value = 1 }
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_core_dirty_mlr = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_abort_dirty_campaign = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_campaign_target value = 4 compare = equals }
			OR = {
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_holds_ert_playable = yes
				AND = { country_exists = ERT has_war_with = ERT ERT = { has_capitulated = yes capital_scope = { OR = { is_controlled_by = RUS controller = { is_subject_of = RUS has_war_with = ERT } } } } }
			}
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_annex_dirty_target = yes
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_ert_absorbed
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_ert_counted } }
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_ert_counted
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_dirty_neighbors_taken value = 1 }
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_core_dirty_ert = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_abort_dirty_campaign = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_campaign_target value = 5 compare = equals }
			OR = {
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_holds_irt_playable = yes
				AND = { country_exists = IRT has_war_with = IRT IRT = { has_capitulated = yes capital_scope = { OR = { is_controlled_by = RUS controller = { is_subject_of = RUS has_war_with = IRT } } } } }
			}
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_annex_dirty_target = yes
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_irt_absorbed
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_irt_counted } }
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_irt_counted
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_dirty_neighbors_taken value = 1 }
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_core_dirty_irt = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_abort_dirty_campaign = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_campaign_target value = 6 compare = equals }
			OR = {
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_holds_sca_playable = yes
				AND = { country_exists = SCA has_war_with = SCA SCA = { has_capitulated = yes capital_scope = { OR = { is_controlled_by = RUS controller = { is_subject_of = RUS has_war_with = SCA } } } } }
			}
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_annex_dirty_target = yes
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_sca_absorbed
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_sca_counted } }
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_sca_counted
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_dirty_neighbors_taken value = 1 }
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_core_dirty_sca = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_abort_dirty_campaign = yes
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_check_rus_dirty_campaign = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = RUS
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_last_empire_proclaimed }
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_release_lost_absorptions = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_dirty_campaign_active }
			if = {
				limit = { has_capitulated = yes }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_abort_dirty_campaign = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_current_target_ready = yes }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_settle_dirty_target = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_campaign_target_still_open = yes }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_abort_dirty_campaign = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_proclaim_last_empire = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = RUS
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_last_empire_proclaimed }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_has_empire_territory = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_last_empire_proclaimed
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_dirty_campaign_active
		clear_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_campaign_target
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = RUS_national_spirit }
			swap_ideas = { remove_idea = RUS_national_spirit add_idea = RUS_last_empire_spirit }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_idea = RUS_last_empire_spirit } }
			add_ideas = RUS_last_empire_spirit
		}
		set_cosmetic_tag = RUS_last_empire
		set_politics = { ruling_party = chauvinism elections_allowed = no }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

# Coalition membership survives the replacement of the claimant country object.


# Country-scoped reconciliation; no world scan on diplomacy hooks.
